- **h22-docsurgeon**: G7 public-claim-surface surgeon. Never touches code, tests, or state files. ([JosephOIbrahim/Synapse](https://github.com/JosephOIbrahim/Synapse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/h22-docsurgeon.md) · ★10)
- **h22-forge**: Implementation agent for gated H22 blueprint work — G1 port waves, G5 grounding tools, G9 gate build, baseline/freeze code. Refuses any dispatch that lacks a GATEWARDEN ALLOW. - **h22-gatewarden**: Read-only gate-state oracle for the H22 blueprint harness. ([JosephOIbrahim/Synapse](https://github.com/JosephOIbrahim/Synapse/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/h22-gatewarden.md) · ★10)
